  - id: Sda0afbed2cf43c3a
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA.md
    okf_sha256: b882b23f2e83f9c6876fe655e690ee5b8fc4c110053453eeeea7dbf13ad79097
    heading: Key Detail 12 — Physical Submission of Original Tender Security
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 7
    quote: '- **Original Security Documents:** Bank Guarantee, Insurance Surety Bond, Demand Draft or Banker’s Cheque
      towards Tender Security (EMD).

      - **Submission Deadline:** 16.10.2026 up to 18.00 Hrs (IST).

      - **Submission Mode:** In Person / By Post / By Courier.

      - **Recipient and Venue:** General Manager (Contracts), Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited, 3rd Floor,
      BMTC Complex, Shanthinagar, Bengaluru-560027, Karnataka, India.

    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf

    json_pointer: /context_fragments/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_visual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 34,\n  \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n}"

- kind: summary
  text: To provide tenderers with reasonable preparation time after issuing addenda or corrigenda, the Employer
    may exercise discretion to extend the tender submission deadline in accordance with ITT Clause 15.0.
  evidence_ids:
  - J8491194dd6f4d9ec
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C84c44e5d5c3898e1
  citations:
  - id: J8491194dd6f4d9ec
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e939aa5d764ea69ebc90821e3739717055c6fc9a79f67e3ed96823ded78b04f9
    heading: Clause 7.0 — Amendment to Tender Documents
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 13
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/6
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"7.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"\
      7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\\n\\n  7.1        Before the deadline for submission of Tenders, the\
      \ Employer may issue further instructions\\n               to Tenderers or any modifications to existing Tender\
      \ documents in the form of an addendum\\n         & corrigendum. Such an amendment in the form of an addendum/corrigendum\
      \ will be made\\n              available at the eProcurement Portal. Bidders are advised to visit the Portal\
      \ frequently.\\n\\n  7.2        Without prejudice to the order of preference as specified in Clause 1.5 of\
      \ the General\\n             Conditions of Contract, the provisions in such addenda and/or corrigenda shall\
      \ take priority\\n             over the Tender Documents issued previously.\\n\\n  7.3      To afford Tenderers\
      \ reasonable time for preparing their Tenders after considering such\\n           amendments, the Employer\
      \ may, at his discretion, extend the deadline for the submission of\\n             tenders in accordance with\
      \ Clause 15.0 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"7.0     AMENDMENT\
      \ TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match
  created_at: '2026-11-01T06:32:09+00:00'
  status: draft
- kind: summary
  text: No modifications or withdrawals of tender are permitted after the official deadline for submission, and
    post-deadline withdrawal triggers actions under ITT Clause 13.7.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jfb725f509e473676
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: Cdf6f7d7b0109d2a1
  citations:
  - id: Jfb725f509e473676
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e939aa5d764ea69ebc90821e3739717055c6fc9a79f67e3ed96823ded78b04f9
    heading: Clause 17.0 — Modification and Withdrawal of Tenders
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 19
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/16
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"17.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\
      17.0     MODIFICATION AND WITHDRAWAL OF TENDERS\\n\\n  17.1      Except where expressly permitted by these\
      \ Instructions, the Tenderer shall not make or cause\\n           any alteration, erasure, or obliteration\
      \ to the text of the documents prepared by the Employer\\n           and submitted by the Tenderer with or\
      \ as part of his Tender.\\n\\n  17.2     No modifications or withdrawal of tender shall be permitted after\
      \ the deadline for submission\\n               of the Tender. Withdrawal of the Tender after the deadline\
      \ for submission of Tenders but\\n             before the period of Tender Validity shall result in Employer’s\
      \ action as per Clause 13.7 of\\n               Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  17.3     The Tender\
      \ submitted online will be taken as the final bid.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"17.0     MODIFICATION\
      \ AND WITHDRAWAL OF TENDERS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match
  created_at: '2026-11-01T06:32:09+00:00'
  status: draft

### J2d79831d07f43931

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]

> {
>   "pdf_page": 34,
>   "text": "The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \nInvitation. \n8) \nIn case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other Government Body, Contract \nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS) deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant. \n9) \nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be considered. \n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification criteria: \nSl. No \nCriteria Requirement \nDocument Submission \nrequirement \n3.1.B (i) \nLiquidity: Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \navailability of credit facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \nfrom banks for meeting the fund requirement etc.). \nAttachment-13 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n3.1.B (ii) \nAnnual Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \nto 2025-2026) achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \nclasses of civil engineering construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \nAttachment-8 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n      Notes: \n1) \nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity) needs to be uploaded in the \nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \n2) \nAnnual financial statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial years \n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer must be certified either by the \nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \nhis stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number. \n3) \nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \nin the Audited Balance sheet will be considered."
> }

>       "text": "7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\n\n  7.1        Before the deadline for submission of Tenders, the Employer may issue further instructions\n               to Tenderers or any modifications to existing Tender documents in the form of an addendum\n         & corrigendum. Such an amendment in the form of an addendum/corrigendum will be made\n              available at the eProcurement Portal. Bidders are advised to visit the Portal frequently.\n\n  7.2        Without prejudice to the order of preference as specified in Clause 1.5 of the General\n             Conditions of Contract, the provisions in such addenda and/or corrigenda shall take priority\n             over the Tender Documents issued previously.\n\n  7.3      To afford Tenderers reasonable time for preparing their Tenders after considering such\n           amendments, the Employer may, at his discretion, extend the deadline for the submission of\n             tenders in accordance with Clause 15.0 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT)."

Withdrawal of the Tender after the deadline for submission of Tenders but\n             before the period of Tender Validity shall result in Employer’s action as per Clause 13.7 of\n               Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\n\n  17.3     The Tender submitted online will be taken as the final bid."
